#2418. Portuguese football around the web: from Port Vale to Seixal

Simon Curtis takes a look at "the quiet man at the helm of a national institution". #2417. Les Amazones reçues 5 sur 5

Désormais seules en tête de l'Arkema Seconde Ligue, les Amazones se déplaçaient ce dimanche à Lille où elles se sont imposées 1-0 grâce à une Ithaisa Vinoly impériale dans les buts et un but tardif d'Héloïse Mansuy. Un cinquième succès consécutif en championnat qui permet à l'ASSE de conforter sa place de leader et de semer la concurrence !Après leur victoire convaincante face à Auxerre (2-0), les Vertes faisaient face dans le Nord à un concurrent sérieux dans la course à la montée et Yannick Chandioux devait en plus composer sans Rachel Corboz et Agathe Felden, absentes surprises de dernière minute.Sur la pelouse synthétique du LOSC, ce qui avait obligé les Stéphanoises à s'entraîner toute la semaine sur une surface identique à Saint-Priest-en-Jarez, les Vertes ne connaissaient pourtant pas l'entame espérée, les Lilloises imposant rapidement leur rythme et se montrant particulièrement agressives dans les duels. Dès la 9e minute, la meilleure buteuse de D2, Coralie Monguillon se présentait face à Ithaisa Vinoly, mais la gardienne espagnole remportait son face-à-face et préservait les cages stéphanoises.Le LOSC continuait de pousser et Wissem Bouzid voyait ensuite une nouvelle tentative dangereuse contrée in extremis avant que Chloé Neller ne trouve le poteau à quelques minutes de la pause. Côté stéphanois, seule Sarah Cambot parvenait à répondre sur une belle reprise de volée qui frôlait le montant adverse. A la pause, les Vertes pouvaient s'estimer heureuses de ne pas être menées.Mais au retour des vestiaires, les Amazones affichaient de meilleures intentions et se montraient davantage entreprenantes, même si les Lilloises continuaient de poser de sérieux problèmes à l'arrière-garde verte. À l'heure de jeu, un énorme cafouillage dans la surface stéphanoise faisait même passer un sérieux frisson dans le camp forézien avant de finir en corner.Les minutes défilaient et les joueuses de Yannick Chandioux peinaient toujours à prendre véritablement le contrôle de la rencontre. À la 74e minute, Coralie Monguillon se heurtait encore à une Ithaisa Vinoly exceptionnelle. La portière espagnole réalisait une parade déterminante et permettait aux siennes de rester dans le match.Et ce ne fut pas en vain car à 10 minutes de la fin du match, sur un coup de pied arrêté, un ballon traînait dans les cinq mètres cinquante nordistes et Héloïse Mansuy surgissait pour le pousser au fond des filets. Un avantage aussi précieux qu'inattendu (0-1, 80e) !Les dernières minutes devenaient alors particulièrement tendues, les Lilloises vexées tentant de revenir au score par tous les moyens possibles, faisant progressivement monter la nervosité entre les deux bancs.Sans surprise, la tension dégénéra et entraîna une pluie de cartons à la 90e minute, l'entraîneur lillois Mathieu Robail et la buteuse Coralie Monguillon étant ainsi expulsés mais malgré cette fin de rencontre particulièrement agitée, les Vertes ne lâchaient rien et conservaient leur avantage jusqu'au coup de sifflet final.Un précieux succès, acquis dans la douleur, qui permettait aux Amazones d'enchaîner une cinquième victoire consécutive en championnat et de prolonger leur invincibilité.Yannick Chandioux: "On retiendra avant tout la victoire. Et celle-ci, on la doit en grande partie à notre gardienne, Ithaïsa. Elle nous a maintenues dans le match pendant longtemps, alors que nous sommes passées à côté de notre entame, quasiment dès les premières minutes. Nous avons été moins agressives que les Lilloises, qui ont pleinement joué sur cet aspect et nous ont mises en difficulté (...) Ne pas avoir encaissé de but dans cette rencontre relève presque du petit hold-up. (...) Parfois, les saisons réussies se construisent aussi dans ce genre de matchs, que l'on parvient à gagner alors que peu de monde nous voyait l'emporter".Avec 17 points au compteur, les Stéphanoises conservent bien sûr leur fauteuil de leader avec deux longueurs d'avance sur Le Mans et trois sur Roubaix. Elles retrouveront leur public samedi prochain au stade Salif-Keita face à Nice, l'actuel 10e... d'un championnat à 11

#2416. Lazio e Gattuso, da 45,4 milioni spesi a 105 stimati per cinque giocatori

Noslin conta già quattro gol e quattro assist in 11 partite; per Isaksen la valutazione ipotizzata è di 30 milioni.

La Lazio avrebbe speso 45,4 milioni di euro per cinque giocatori che oggi vengono stimati complessivamente circa 105 milioni. Il confronto riguarda Tijjani Noslin, Gustav Isaksen, Reda Belahyane, Oliver Provstgaard e Romano Floriani Mussolini. Secondo quanto riportato da TUTTOmercatoWEB, La cifra d’acquisto presuppone che siano maturate tutte le condizioni per il pagamento dei bonus; quella attuale è una valutazione ipotetica, non il prezzo di cessioni concordate.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

La crescita dei cinque viene collegata anche al lavoro di Gennaro Gattuso. La Lazio capolista è descritta come una squadra la cui forza sta soprattutto nel gruppo e nella volontà di vincere insieme, seguendo la richiesta del tecnico. Gattuso non si è lamentato dei giocatori a disposizione e ha lavorato per migliorare anche chi in passato aveva deluso le aspettative. Ai progressi dei singoli si aggiunge la fiducia ricevuta dall’allenatore.

Il cambiamento più evidente riguarda Reda Belahyane. In estate il centrocampista non riusciva neppure a completare gli allenamenti e arrivava da stagioni difficili. Ora è considerato un elemento insostituibile nel centrocampo della squadra che guida la Serie A.

Anche Tijjani Noslin ha migliorato il proprio rendimento. In 11 partite ha realizzato quattro gol e fornito quattro assist, facendo già meglio rispetto ai due precedenti campionati disputati nella Capitale. Il confronto tra la Lazio della scorsa stagione e quella attuale mette in risalto quanto sia cambiato il contributo di giocatori come loro.

Oliver Provstgaard è diventato un punto fermo della difesa e ha conquistato una titolarità che prima non aveva avuto in biancoceleste. Per Gustav Isaksen, invece, non erano in discussione classe e qualità, ma la continuità. Gattuso lo ha aspettato quando non era nelle condizioni migliori, poi lo ha inserito lasciandogli ampia libertà di creare.

Il percorso di Romano Floriani Mussolini parte dalla crescita nella Lazio e passa attraverso i prestiti. Alla Cremonese, retrocessa in Serie B, era stato spesso una riserva; oggi occupa stabilmente la fascia destra all’Olimpico.

Da questa selezione restano fuori Mandas, Frattesi, Taylor e Zaccagni. Nella valutazione proposta, per il portiere viene attribuito un ruolo più importante al lavoro dello staff. Per gli altri tre, le qualità già possedute avrebbero reso più semplice l’intervento di Gattuso.

#2413. Stuttgart lower asking price for Newcastle- and Galatasaray-linked attacker

After Bilal El Khannouss' loan move from Leicester City to VfB Stuttgart was made permanent for €15 million last summer, the 22-year-old could have left the Bundesliga club again almost immediately.

However, Stuttgart were reportedly demanding €65 million to let him go – a fee that proved too high for interested parties such as Galatasaray, Newcastle United and Al Ittihad.

Now, according to Sky Germany, the Morocco international is available at a significantly reduced price, with the German broadcaster reporting that his new valuation stands at €40 million, including bonuses.

A move could potentially be discussed again as early as January, with Sky reporting that Galatasaray remain interested in signing him.

El Khannouss' contract with Stuttgart runs until the summer of 2030.

#2411. Contribute to help victims of the Pedrógão Grande tragedy

Forest fires occur every summer in Portugal but the tragedy that struck the community of Pedrógão Grande in the centre of the country is unprecedented. Over 60 people lost their lives, many trapped in their cars when trying to flee from the flames.

This Just Giving page is a simple way for people, wherever they are in the world, to make a contribution to help rebuild these communities and bring some comfort to those who lost everything in the blink of an eye. The money raised will be sent to a network of local authorities and charities working in the area affected. Thank you for your support.

#2410. PSV hold on to book narrow Champions League win in Porto

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

PSV survived a second-half onslaught in Porto to secure their first win of the Champions League group stages.

Guus Til scored the only goal when he tapped in from close range after Esmir Bajraktarevic’s shot had come back off the post and was steered across goal by Sven Mijnans.

The Eindhoven club were worth their narrow lead against Francesco Farioli’s side after dominating possession in the first half, but rarely troubled Diogo Costa in the Porto goal.

Til went close with a header early in the second half before putting PSV ahead in the 57th minute.

But the visitors had to defend heroically as Porto poured forward in search of an equaliser, aided by an increasingly fragile Matej Kovar.

The goalkeeper put his defenders in jeopardy with a weak clearance in the 72nd minute, but redeemed himself by saving Victor Froholdt’s shot with his chest before Lutsarel Geertruida blocked the follow-up from Pepê.

A minute later Kovar was left stranded after he rushed out of his penalty area, but Sergiño Dest managed to hack the ball away before Alberto Costa could poke it into the open goal.

At the other end, substitute Denis Man came close to scoring a second for PSV with his first touch when his curling shot beat Costa but failed to sneak inside the post.

The 1-0 win lifts PSV to a provisional 16th place in the Champions League table at the halfway stage of round three, while Porto are still looking for their first point.

Their next match is at home to Belgian side Club Brugge, who will make the short trip to Eindhoven on November 4.

#2409. Doveri vince il premio Bulgarelli, premiato anche Grassadonia della Lazio

Il tecnico biancoceleste riceve il riconoscimento per il calcio femminile; l’arbitro parla ai giovani colleghi.

Daniele Doveri ha ricevuto il premio Bulgarelli Number 8 come miglior arbitro. Tra i vincitori della stagione 2025-2026 c’è anche Gianluca Grassadonia della Lazio, premiato tra gli allenatori per il calcio femminile. Secondo quanto riportato da SportMediaset, La cerimonia si svolge al Comune di Bologna.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Ricevendo il riconoscimento, Doveri ha ricordato di essere l’arbitro con più presenze in Serie A, oltre che il più esperto e il più anziano anagraficamente. Per questo considera suo compito «dare l'esempio sul terreno di gioco» e trasmettere la propria esperienza ai giovani arbitri che si affacciano alla Serie B e alla Serie A, affinché prosegua il processo di rinnovamento.

Il premio porta il nome dello storico numero 8 del Bologna, tra i fondatori dell’Aic-Associazione italiana calciatori, che organizza l’iniziativa. Doveri si è detto orgoglioso di ricevere un riconoscimento intitolato a Bulgarelli. Ha ricordato il calciatore come «una persona estremamente eretta» e ha aggiunto: «recuperare i valori di una volta e proiettarli nel futuro è la chiave».

Per il calcio maschile, il premio tra gli allenatori è andato a Cesc Fàbregas del Como, intervenuto con un videomessaggio di ringraziamento. Grassadonia è stato invece scelto per il calcio femminile.

Tra i calciatori sono stati premiati Kenan Yildiz della Juventus e Martina Rosucci, rispettivamente come miglior calciatore e miglior calciatrice. Rosucci ha inviato al pubblico un videomessaggio. Alla cerimonia sono presenti Fabio Capello, presidente del premio, Umberto Calcagno, presidente dell’Aic, Maurizio Fabbri, presidente dell’Assemblea legislativa dell’Emilia-Romagna, e Roberta Li Calzi, assessora allo Sport del Comune di Bologna.

#2406. Vincent Kompany returns to Bayern Munich training ground after illness

Bayern Munich head coach Vincent Kompany was reportedly back at the club's training ground at Säbener Straße on Tuesday after being absent due to illness since collapsing on October 23.

According to TZ, Kompany was sitting at his computer in his office at the club's headquarters shortly after 10am.

The 40-year-old Belgian coach was greeted by assistant Floribert Ngalula and later held a longer conversation with sporting director Christoph Freund.

From his office, Kompany was also able to watch injured players such as Luis Diaz and Jamal Musiala continue their rehabilitation training on the pitch.

Kompany had been absent since collapsing on the training pitch on October 23, two days before Bayern Munich's 3-1 Bundesliga win over SC Freiburg.

The 40-year-old was subsequently diagnosed with meningeal irritation and continued his recovery at home after being discharged from hospital.

Bayern assistant Aaron Danks was in charge for the five games Kompany missed.

Kompany is expected to return to the touchline when Bayern Munich face 1. FC Köln in the Bundesliga on November 20, following the international break.

#2404. Lazio e Roma non lo confermarono: D'Uffizi dall’Ascoli alla Nazionale

Il 22enne è stato convocato nell’Under 21 per le amichevoli con Francia e Belgio dopo l’indisponibilità di Vavassori.

Simone D'Uffizi è stato convocato per la prima volta nella Nazionale Under 21. L’8 novembre 2026 Silvio Baldini lo ha chiamato al raduno di Tirrenia, dopo l’indisponibilità di Dominic Vavassori, per le amichevoli contro Francia e Belgio. Secondo quanto riportato da Gazzetta Regionale, Oggi il 22enne è un punto di riferimento dell’Ascoli; da bambino, invece, Roma e Lazio non lo avevano confermato perché ritenevano troppo esile la sua struttura fisica.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

D'Uffizi muove i primi passi nella Breda, società romana in cui si mette presto in evidenza. A nove anni attira gli osservatori della Roma, ma il padre preferisce aspettare. L’ingresso nel vivaio giallorosso arriva due anni dopo, anche grazie all’interessamento di Bruno Conti.

L’esperienza alla Roma si interrompe presto. D'Uffizi passa alla Lazio, ritrova Leonardo Fratangeli e indossa anche la fascia da capitano. Neppure il club biancoceleste, però, lo conferma: il ragazzo torna così alla Breda, la squadra da cui era partito.

Nell’ottobre 2026, ripensando a quel periodo, l’attaccante ha raccontato di aver vissuto entrambe le esclusioni come colpi difficili da assorbire. Nella sua ricostruzione, a pesare era soprattutto il fisico ancora troppo esile. Dopo il ritorno alla Breda aveva persino pensato di smettere, incerto che il calcio potesse restare qualcosa di più di un divertimento.

Il cammino prosegue lontano dai vivai di Roma e Lazio. D'Uffizi passa alla Lodigiani e poi alla Nuova Florida, in Serie D, dove comincia a misurarsi con un calcio diverso da quello giovanile. Nel luglio 2021 firma con la Viterbese ed entra tra i professionisti.

Esterno classe 2004 di scuola Lodigiani, in una sfida contro la prima squadra della Fidelis Andria entra a gara in corso e segna due gol in cinque minuti. È un altro episodio del percorso iniziato dopo il ritorno alla Breda.

Feyenoord endured a frustrating evening in Spain as a penalty by Real Betis’s outstanding player, Isco, secured a 1-0 win for the La Liga side.

#2401. Top five football betting tips and tricks

Football is the world’s most famous sport, and it is the sport of choice of a great many sports punters as well. Especially popular in Europe, betting on football can be an incredibly profitable enterprise, so long as you know how to do it.

Getting started on football betting is far from easy, though. This form of gambling requires dedication, investment, and persistence. As you gain experience, your intuition will improve and you will be able to make more correct predictions.

But experience is not the only thing that can help you become a better football punter. In fact, you can substantially improve your betting strategy by simply following some basic football betting tips.

You might find shopping around boring and unhelpful, but you just have to do it when you search sites for football betting. As you know, the higher the odds, the bigger the payout. What you may have missed, however, is that odds are different at each bookmaker. Having said that, shopping around for the best odds is sensible, as it rewards you with bigger payouts.

In this line of thought, sticking to one bookmaker is virtually pointless. Even if the bookie of your choosing offers the best odds for a number of matches, it is highly unlikely that their odds will remain the best for long.

An obvious one, we know. And yet, you will be surprised by the number of football bettors who wager on football, but do not follow the sport. So, why is it important to follow the action closely?

First of all, by watching football matches, you can take part in the most exciting football betting market – the live betting market. What is more important, however, is that following the action provides an invaluable insight into teams and players’ style, condition, and determination – important factors  to consider when placing a bet.

As you watch more and more games, you will improve your analytic abilities. In time, you will be able to make more accurate predictions about the outcome of different matches, which, of course,  will result in placing more winning bets.

As you know, odds are based on the probability of occurrence of a certain outcome; the higher the odds, the more unlikely it is for that outcome to actually occur. But what happens when the odds offered by the bookmaker represent a probability that is lower than the actual probability of an outcome occurring? In such a situation, you can place what is called a value bet.

If you want to become a professional football punter, you should always search for value when betting, as it leads to huge profits

Favoured by beginners in particular, accumulators are a type of football wager which allows you to bet on as many fixtures as you like. Most commonly, you will be asked to choose a match winner, but as the market is diverse, you may be offered a number of other betting options. What makes accumulators an appealing betting market is the huge potential payout – here, you can stake as little as £1 and have the potential to win more than £500,000, depending on the bookmaker and the number of selected fixtures.

If you find accumulators’ payouts too good to be true, you are right. As a matter of fact, there is a reason for the odds to be set so high – actually winning an accumulator bet, especially one which involves a wide selection of matches, is nearly impossible. You see, all you need for your whole wager to lose is one wrong prediction. For example, if you bet on 10 matches and just one of them goes wrong, your entire accumulator is lost.

To make money by betting on football, avoid betting on accumulators – the risk is simply not worth it.

Finally, if you want to be a successful football punter, learn how not to mix emotions with betting. This is easier said than done, though.

Whenever your favourite team plays, you want them to win, regardless of how (im)possible that may be. If you do not learn how to leave emotions out of betting, however, you are highly likely to put money on your favourite team even if they have no chance of winning the match. Conversely, should you base your bets on hard facts and not on emotions, you increase your chances to win.

Our advice – avoid betting on fixtures that involve one of your favourite teams, that is the easiest way to keep emotions at bay.

#2400. U15 : de la joie contre les Caladois

Les U15 de Benjamin Petit pouvaient être heureux à la fin de leur match contre les Caladois du FC Villefranche Beaujolais. A l'issue d'une rencontre très disputée, comme souvent dans cet exigeant championnat, ils n'ont rien lâché face à un adversaire de valeur et ont su trouver les ressources pour l'emporter. Cela récompense leur volonté d'aller de l'avant, leur ténacité et leur concentration dans l'aspect défensif. Sans quelques joueurs retenus avec les U16 de Kevin De Jesus (Ilan Clapeyron, Riad Moutae, Kaïs Boukerma et Hugo Durin) tous ont su se mettre au diapason, et cette victoire est celle d'un collectif très solidaire.

L'entame du match a été stéphanoise, les Verts s'installant dans le camp caladois. Dès la 9ème minute, sur un centre venu de la gauche, Nolan Lebreton remettait pour Charly Davignon qui était devancé de peu par un défenseur. Deux minutes plus tard, un coup-franc de ce même Nolan Lebreton était capté par le gardien. Les Verts monopolisaient le ballon mais le dernier geste, souvent dans les petits espaces, manquait de justesse pour mettre de conclure. Peu à peu, Villefranche desserrait l'étreinte stéphanoise et se montrait dangereux sur coup de pied arrêté, mais Manoa Frison faisait bonne garde, notamment, à la 26ème, où il réalisait une double parade avant que Victor Hamouma ne s'interpose pour stopper la tentative adverse. Quelques minutes plus tard, Charly Davignon, qui repiquait au centre, était victime d'une faute laquelle donnait lieu à un coup-franc. Nolhan Fernandes le frappait et mettait le gardien à contribution. Peu après sur un long centre de Nolhan Fernandes, Hugo Bastide obtenait un corner. Ce corner, joué à deux, permettait à Nolan Lebreton d'adresser un long centre au deuxième poteau que le portier caladois dégageait. Les contres de Villefranche butaient sur une solide défense stéphanoise, concentrée et rigoureuse qui gérait bien les espaces et s'attachait à ressortir proprement les ballons. Le score était donc nul et vierge au repos.

La deuxième mi-temps repartait sur les mêmes bases, avec une maitrîse verte retrouvée et des situations intéressantes, sans parvenir toutefois à faire la différence.  Mais les Verts, suite à un carton blanc à la 54ème minute, se retrouvaient réduits à 10 pendant une dizaine de minutes, et ce fait de jeu freinait un peu leur dynamique. Ils allaient trouver la faille suite à un cafouillage dans la surface, avec une main adverse et un accrochage sur Mathis La Rosa. Toujours est-il que l'arbitre sanctionnait cette action d'un penalty que Charly Davignon transformait, donnant l'avantage aux Verts (68ème). La fin du match allait être plutôt hâchée avec plusieurs coups-de-pied arrêtés que Villefranche jouait directement. Les Verts se créaient cependant quelques opportunités mais un manque de justesse ne leur permettait pas de les concrétiser. Le match se terminait donc sur cette victoire stéphanoise somme toute logique, tant elle avait été bien construite.

La composition : Frison - Bergougnoux (Uneau, 50ème), L. La Rosa, Hamouma, Bastide - Wojcik (Davignon, 50ème), M.La Rosa, Lebreton - Fernandes (Madjidi, 50ème), Maganga Verrier, Davignon (Linganzi, 41ème).

La réaction de Benjamin Petit : "Le match a été serré, très disputé face à une bonne équipe de Villefranche, comme souvent dans ce championnat. Il n'y a pas de match facile et c'est un bon apprentissage pour les jeunes. Nous avons fait vingt premières minutes plutôt intéressantes, avec de la maîtrise, une volonté de défendre en avançant, un point que travaillons. Nous avons baissé en fin de première mi-temps, avec plus de difficulté à maintenir notre pression, ce qui nous a menés à défendre plus bas. En conclusion, je dirais que globalement, nous avons eu la maîtrise, mais mon regret est le manque de justesse, de percussion et d'efficacité pour créer du danger sur le but adverse et se mettre à l'abri. Je retiens notre solidité défensive, notre rigueur dans les prises en charge, notre détermination à ne rien encaisser, notamment sur les coups-de-pied arrêtés, un domaine dans lequel nous avons progressé. Il faudra néanmoins éviter certaines fautes, évitables, qui offrent des situations dangereuses à l'adversaire. C'était un match intéressant dans l'apprentissage, la gestion des temps forts et des temps faibles. Il faut toutefois  essayer d'être plus efficace dans nos temps forts tout en restant solides dans les temps faibles."

#2398. Bundesliga and NFL extend partnership to strengthen global collaboration

Having first entered into a partnership in 2022, the Bundesliga and the National Football League (NFL) have extended their Memorandum of Understanding (MoU), reaffirming their commitment to working together on cross-sport initiatives and creating new fan experiences worldwide.

The extension will see the two leagues continue to collaborate in key areas such as innovation, digital and experiential marketing as well as cross-sport content opportunities, while sharing best practices and exploring new ways to engage fans globally.

"The renewal of our MOU with the NFL is a pivotal step in the Bundesliga’s continued expansion in the United States,” said Bundesliga CEO Steffen Merkel.

“This collaboration not only deepens the connection between the NFL and Bundesliga but also plays a key role in our overall growth strategy in the US market.”

As part of the partnership, the NFL has staged five regular-season games in German football stadiums over the past four years, with matches taking place in Munich (FC Bayern München), Frankfurt (Eintracht Frankfurt) and Berlin (Hertha BSC).

In the United States, the Bundesliga and the NFL have collaborated on a number of physical and digital events. For example, former Bayern Munich star Rafinha joined ex-New England Patriots players Brian Hoyer and Sebastian Vollmer for a watch party ahead of the Patriots' home game against the Buffalo Bills in December.

Elsewhere, Borussia Dortmund legend Patrick Owomoyela announced the Pittsburgh Steelers' sixth-round selection at the 2026 NFL Draft in the Steel City.

“The partnership between the NFL and the Bundesliga demonstrates the value of bringing two of the world’s leading sports leagues together,” explained NFL Commissioner Roger Goodell.

“By extending our collaboration, we can continue to learn from each other, create new experiences for fans and explore innovative ways to grow our sports in Germany, the United States and around the world.”

Other initiatives have included NFL legend Adam Vinatieri setting an official Guinness World Record by kicking a field goal in the Swiss Alps at an altitude of 3,454 metres, with former Bayern Munich player Giovane Elber serving as the holder.

The Bundesliga has also been represented at the last two Super Bowls, with former players Claudio Pizarro and Roman Weidenfeller attending the events in New Orleans and San Francisco.

The Bundesliga's long-term US growth strategy is supported by a 17-year partnership with Relevent Sports. The agreement includes media rights, marketing and events, regional sponsorships, localised content and grassroots initiatives.

Feyenoord preserved their four-point advantage over champions PSV after coming away with a point from Eindhoven thanks to an improved second-half performance.

Telstar lost by the same margin at Go Ahead Eagles, whose striker Søren Tengstedt netted twice in the first half to lead the Eredivisie scoring charts with eight goals.

#2395. Calò e la mancata convocazione in Nazionale, Mancini: "quando servirà lo faremo ma non perché ce lo dicono i procuratori"

Il ct riconosce le prestazioni del centrocampista del Frosinone, ma chiarisce il criterio seguito per le convocazioni.

Giacomo Calò è seguito dalla Nazionale, ma Roberto Mancini non lo ha convocato per il raduno di Coverciano. Il commissario tecnico riconosce il buon momento del centrocampista del Frosinone, allenato da Massimiliano Alvini. Secondo quanto riportato da figc, A 29 anni, però, Calò non rientra tra i giocatori che il ct vuole osservare in questa fase, dedicata soprattutto ai più giovani.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Gli Azzurri si sono ritrovati a Coverciano la sera dell’8 novembre 2026. Nel pomeriggio del 9 novembre è prevista la prima seduta di allenamento verso le ultime due partite del girone di Nations League, contro Francia e Belgio. Nella conferenza stampa di inizio raduno, Mancini ha affrontato anche il tema delle convocazioni e delle prospettive della squadra. La FIGC riporta le sue dichiarazioni.

Mancini considera Calò uno dei giocatori che stanno facendo bene e che la Nazionale tiene sotto osservazione. Il ct ha aggiunto che nel Frosinone ci sono altri tre o quattro elementi validi. La scelta per questo raduno, tuttavia, segue un criterio diverso: «Ma non possiamo chiamare giocatori di 29/30 anni in questa fase, quando servirà lo faremo ma non perché ce lo dicono i procuratori».

Il tecnico ha allargato il discorso allo spazio concesso ai giovani in Serie A. A suo giudizio, la situazione è rimasta più o meno la stessa: alcuni club li impiegano, altri no. Per la Nazionale, ha spiegato, resta l’obbligo di cercare ragazzi da convocare e conoscere direttamente, anche quando sono molto giovani. L’intento è valutarne le qualità tecniche in vista della loro possibile crescita.

La linea seguita dal ct riguarda anche alcuni campioni d’Europa assenti dall’elenco, tra cui Bernardeschi, Chiesa e Cristante. Mancini conosce già Bernardeschi e Cristante e ha detto che potrà richiamarli quando ne avrà bisogno. Con Chiesa ha parlato più volte, anche di recente: il giocatore ora non sta giocando, deve ritrovare la condizione e, secondo il ct, a 29 anni può tornare presto in Nazionale.

Mancini ha confermato sei dei nove volti nuovi visti nella finestra di settembre e ottobre: Ghilardi, Kayode, Romano, Doumbia, Sebastiano Esposito e Vergara. Ha richiamato Ahanor e accolto dall’Under 21 Pisilli e Koleosho. Parlando del loro passaggio, ha ricordato il buon rapporto con Baldini e gli ha augurato di vincere l’Europeo.

Tra le prime convocazioni figurano due giocatori del Como di Fabregas: Mattia Liberali, classe 2007, e Riccardo Cassano, classe 2009. Nell’elenco c’è anche Alphadjo Cisse, attaccante del Milan nato nel 2006. La sua chiamata era nell’aria: l’8 novembre ha segnato per la quinta volta in Serie A ed è il miglior marcatore rossonero. Completano le novità il difensore dell’Udinese Matteo Palma e Simone Lontani, del Parma, entrambi classe 2008.

Palma, Liberali e Cisse resteranno con il gruppo per tutto il raduno. Cassano e Lontani si alleneranno per alcuni giorni a Coverciano, poi raggiungeranno rispettivamente le Nazionali Under 18 e Under 19. La prima è impegnata nel Mondiale al via il 19 novembre in Qatar; la seconda nella fase delle qualificazioni europee in programma dall’11 al 17 novembre nelle Marche. Mancini li ha chiamati anche per conoscerli e osservarli da vicino.

Il ct ha detto che tutti i giovani presenti gli hanno dato buone sensazioni. Di Liberali ha sottolineato le qualità e il percorso nelle giovanili, pur ricordando le poche presenze in Serie A. Lontani lo aveva colpito già alla prima di campionato: a suo giudizio è migliorato partita dopo partita e il gol contro l’Atalanta ne conferma personalità e doti tecniche.

Palma, che era in bilico tra Italia e Germania, può diventare secondo Mancini un difensore molto bravo. Cisse può ricoprire entrambi i ruoli d’attacco e il ct vuole vederlo al lavoro. Cassano è un regista di centrocampo: deve crescere fisicamente, ma Mancini ne apprezza la lettura del gioco e ritiene che possa diventare importante per il futuro azzurro.

Un’altra scelta tra Italia e Germania riguarda Nicolò Tresoldi. Mancini e Ranieri hanno parlato con lui, ma il ct ha ribadito che la decisione spetta al giocatore. Tresoldi è nato in Italia e suo padre ha giocato per la Nazionale, vincendo l’Europeo Under 21. Per Mancini ci sono possibilità che scelga gli Azzurri, con i quali potrebbe avere un ruolo importante.

#2394. Les U16 n'ont pas fait de détail

Après leur déconvenue d'il y a trois semaines, et la défaite face au CASCOL Oullins à l'extrême limite du temps additionnel (3-2), les U16 de Kevin De Jesus avaient à coeur de réagir. La trêve avait été meublée par une rencontre amicale à Bourg-Peronnas remportée par 3 à 0 (buts de N.Fontaine et Joris Rochette 2), histoire de garder le rythme et de parfaire les automatismes. Les Verts se présentaient hier, sur le terrain synthétique Georges Bereta, avec une équipe dans laquelle figuraient quatre U15 (Ilan Clapeyron, Riad Moutae, Kaïs Boukerma et Hugo Durin) face à la lanterne rouge, l'AFCA (Aurillac Foot Cantal Auvergne) qui, il faut bien l'avouer, n'a pas opposé une farouche résistance aux Verts. Ces derniers l'ont en effet emporté sur le large score de 9 à 0, alors qi'ils menaient déjà 4-0 au repos.

Ce match a ressemblé à une attaque-défense, digne d'un match de handball. Dès la 2ème minute, Hugo Durin se mettait déjà en évidence avec un tir au ras du poteau. Dans la foulée, à la réception d'un ballon venu de la droite, il éliminait son adversaire et marquait  d'un tir au ras du sol. (ASSE-AFCA : 1-0, 3ème).Le jeu se déroulait devant le but aurillacois et Rayan Fontaine, servi par Adem Moussaoui, d'un tir dans un angle fermé aggravait le score. (ASSE-AFCA : 2-0, 7ème)La domination verte ne se relâchait pas : sur un corner, la reprise de la tête de Raphaël Moser était détournée par le gardien (17ème), un coup-franc de Lenny Nebout, tiré à ras de terre, était repoussé par le portier cantalou et la reprise de Noah Fontaine ne trouvait pas le cadre (18ème), un tir de loin de Dixon Ahamadi était une nouvelle fois détourné (22ème). Entre temps, une première incursion aurillacoise était ponctuée par un corner, mais la reprise de la tête n,'inquiétait pas Ilan Clapeyron.  Les Verts allaient de nouveau trouver la faille : à la suite d'une combinaison avec Noah Fontaine, Hugo Durin était trouvé dans un petit espace et sa frappe croisée du gauche faisait mouche. (ASSE-AFCA : 3-0, 24ème).Dès la remise en jeu, le défenseur central Idris Balta s'avançait, plaçait un tir qui rebondissait devant le but et trompait le gardien adverse. (ASSE-AFCA : 4-0, 25ème).A la 30ème minute, suite à un centre de Raphaël Moser, Hugo Durin remisait sur Joris Rochette (photo), mais la reprise de ce dernier était contrée. Aurillac réagissait timidement à la 38ème et sur un tir venu de loin, Ilan Clapeyron se détendait bien. C'était un petit intermède au milieu de la domination stéphanoise qui ne se démentait pas : un coup-franc de Rayan Fontaine trouvait la tête de Dixon Ahamadi, mais le gardien adverse captait le cuir (40ème), une frappe lointaine de Joris Rochette s'écrasait sur l'équerre du but cantalou (43ème), sur une combinaison côté gauche, le tir de Raphaël Moser dans un angle fermé frôlait la transversale (44ème), et, enfin à la 45ème, un tir dévié de Joris Rochette, manquait de tromper le gardien. La mi-temps était sifflée sur ces entrefaites. Le score de 4 à 0 illustrait la mainmise stéphanoise.

Le second acte repartait avec la même physionomie. A la 50ème une subtile combinaison Joris Rochette - Hugo Durin butait sur le gardien. A la 52ème, Joris Rochette repiquait au centre et sa frappe du gauche passait à côté. La minute suivante Hugo Durin alertait Rayan Fontaine et le centre de ce dernier trouvait la tête de son frère Noah, mais sa reprise était détournée en corner, corner sur lequel la tête de Dixon Ahamadi ne trouvait pas le cadre. A la 55ème, Rayan Fontaine était fauché à l'entrée de la surface et le coup-franc de Raphaël Moser s'écrasait sur la transversale aurillacoise. A force d'appuyer, les Verts allaient trouver la faille : une frappe de 25 mètres de Dixon Ahamadi était détournée par le gardien et sur le corner qui suivait Rayan Fontaine trouvait la tête d'Hugo Durin. (ASSE-AFCA : 5-0, 56ème) A la 62ème, Hugo Durin servait latéralement Lenny Nebout mais la frappe de ce dernier était stoppée par le gardien. Servi par une passe verticale d'Idris Balta, Kaïs Boukerma voyait son tir détourné par le portier aurillacois (65ème). A la 69ème, Rayan Fontaine trouvait Hugo Durin dans le dos de la défense et ce dernier concluait du droit. (ASSE-AFCA : 6-0, 69ème).Peu après, Hugo Durin, alerté par Joris Rochette, partait de la ligne médiane, prenait de vitesse la défense adverse et s'en allait marquer du droit. (ASSE-AFCA : 7-0, 72ème).Joris Rochette faisait un festival côté droit et son centre en retrait trouvait Riad Moutae qui  parachevait cette belle action. (ASSE-AFCA : 8-0, 76ème).Enfin, alors qu'il restait une dizaine de minutes à jouer, une belle passe cachée de Lenny Nebout pour Hugo Durin permettait à ce dernier de conclure d'une frappe décroisée entre le gardien et son poteau. C'était son 6ème but de l'après-midi ! (ASSE-AFCA : 9-0, 81ème). Le match à sens unique se terminait sur ce score fleuve qui reflétait bien la sérieuse prestation des jeunes Verts.

La composition : Clapeyron - R.Fontaine, Ahamadi, Balta, Moser - Evaristo, Rochette, Moussaoui (Boukerma, 60ème)- Nebout, Durin, N.Fontaine (Moutae, 60ème).

La réaction de Kevin de Jesus : "Satisfait du match ? Certes, mais mon but c'est de voir des comportements qui progressent, qui avancent vers ce que j'exige des jeunes. A la mi-temps, le résultat semblait acquis, mais j'ai demandé à mes joueurs de continuer à trouver des solutions pour déséquilibrer l'équipe adverse, de varier les combinaisons pour arriver à cet objectif, en diversifiant les circuits, en impliquant toute l'équipe dans cette recherche, défense y compris, par les montées des excentrés dans leur couloir, des centraux dans l'axe pour tenter des frappes de loin. J'ai apprécié le sérieux des joueurs qui sont restés concentrés, sans s'enflammer, en respectant l'adversaire. Cet esprit-là est important."

#2392. Rudi Völler suggests Thomas Müller could follow him into Germany role

Rudi Völler has named former Bayern Munich star Thomas Müller as a potential successor to him as sporting director of the German Football Association (DFB).

The 66-year-old, whose current deal expires in 2028, said on the “Kerners 11” podcast:

“At some point, my time will finally come to an end. And if the DFB – whether [DFB president] Bernd Neuendorf, [DFB vice-president] Aki Watzke or managing director Per Mertesacker – feels that someone else should be brought in, I think that would be a good idea.”

Völler added that he's "absolutely convinced" Müller would be suitable for the role.

"Thomas could certainly do it, just like others could. Whether in my position or another one. They would just have to want it – that's the basis," he said.

Since leaving Bayern Munich at the end of the 2024/25 season, 37-year-old Müller has been playing for Vancouver Whitecaps in Major League Soccer, where his contract runs until the summer of 2027.

#2391. Sports club members becoming less tolerant of homosexuality

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Acceptance of homosexuality in Dutch sports clubs has declined significantly in the last generation, according to a survey of attitudes by the Mulier Institute.

The organisation found that 66% of people had no objection to a fellow member of a sports club being gay, while in 2008 the figure was 85%.

Just over half (55%) of respondents said they had no problem sharing a shower with a gay team-mate of the same sex, which also represented a drop from 73% in 2008.

The Mulier Institute said the findings reflected a social trend that has also been seen in schools, where the proportion of boys aged between 12 and 16 with a positive view of homosexuality has dropped from 40% to 22% since 2021.

The Dutch Olympic Committee NOC*NSF said the findings were “highly regrettable” and pledged to step up its efforts to make sport more inclusive.

But the John Blankenstein Foundation, which campaigns for LGBT emancipation in sport, said it had noticed declining enthusiasm for its workshops and growing numbers of parents who voiced objections.

Karin Blankenstein, who set up the organisation in 2008, told NOS: “I get the sensitivity around transgender people in sport at the top level, but this is about amateur sport.”

#2387. Cancellieri, meno spazio alla Lazio: Fiorentina attenta alla scadenza

Una cessione a gennaio consentirebbe ai biancocelesti di ricavare una somma dal cartellino dell'esterno.

Matteo Cancellieri potrebbe tornare nei piani della Fiorentina per il mercato invernale. Secondo quanto riportato da FiorentinaUno, L'esterno offensivo della Lazio, che nelle ultime settimane ha trovato poco spazio, sarebbe nuovamente seguito da Fabio Paratici. Un'eventuale operazione a gennaio resta un'ipotesi, legata anche alle scelte del club biancoceleste.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il futuro a Roma del calciatore italiano, classe 2002, non appare scontato. Cancellieri non sembra più occupare una posizione centrale nelle scelte tecniche della Lazio e sta attraversando una fase complicata, nella quale giocare con continuità è diventato più difficile.

Alla situazione in campo si aggiunge quella contrattuale: l'accordo con i biancocelesti scade il 30 giugno 2027. Senza un rinnovo, la società dovrà decidere come affrontare gli ultimi mesi del rapporto. Anche il giocatore sarà chiamato a valutare quale soluzione possa offrirgli maggiore continuità.

Paratici conosce il profilo di Cancellieri e potrebbe scegliere tra due possibilità. La prima è provare ad anticipare l'operazione a gennaio, cercando un'intesa con la Lazio. L'altra è aspettare la fine della stagione per tentare di ingaggiare l'esterno a parametro zero.

Attendere consentirebbe alla Fiorentina di non sostenere il costo del cartellino, ma potrebbe esporla alla concorrenza di altre società. Non è la prima volta che il nome di Cancellieri viene associato ai viola: nelle precedenti finestre di mercato il club toscano aveva già mostrato interesse, considerandolo una possibile alternativa per il reparto offensivo.

Tra le caratteristiche prese in considerazione c'è la sua duttilità. Cancellieri può giocare soprattutto sulla corsia destra, ma anche in altre posizioni dell'attacco: una possibilità utile nella costruzione della rosa viola, che potrebbe cambiare volto.

#2386. Opinion: The morality of football

Yesterday evening, Selecao correspondent Nathan Motz accepted a unique opportunity to appear as a guest on the Moral Maze – a BBC radio broadcast dealing with matters of moral and ethical significance.

Is football a moral force for good? That was the question of the day, and each of the four guests were asked to discuss various aspects of the debate from financial fairplay to club takeovers by foreign entities to racism.

Nathan’s segment was concerned with whether football has transcendent moral value, and what that is exactly. Is there a greater significance to the game we are watching, or is it merely 22 humans kicking a leather ball into a net?

Join Nathan as he elaborates on the most significant takeaways of what was a fascinating discussion on the sport and morality in general.

Football, for some, may be the most poignant reminder of the moral universe in which we live. While debate has raged for centuries regarding the precise origin of moral absolutes and how humans ought to live, it is simply unavoidable to say humanity is often troubled by events surrounding the game we all love so dearly.

In not so distant history, every normal act of human life would have been assigned some degree of moral significance. Every action had inherently moral indicators - for good or for evil. Today, postmodern views of morality dominate. These describe morality as an entirely subjective dimension. In other words, it cannot be pinned down exactly whether behaviors are objectively good or bad. We can only find fault with someone else’s interpretation based on their own unique moral code. Morality is relative to the individual or society.

Yet last night’s dialogue concerning football and human behavior was filled with moral accusations and claims which strongly suggested humans are not content with relativist morality. Indeed, we are at times frightened by and disgusted with what we observe in and around football. Our sense of moral justice is deeply disturbed. But with respect to what exactly?

In responding to the panelist’s questions, I observed a familiar paradigm. Each time I have discussed any aspect of moral behavior, the speculation humans are good or at worst morally neutral animals continually reemerges. This widely accepted presupposition is then followed shortly by disgust at some aspect of human behavior. Almost as if to ask “why are all these good humans behaving so badly?” while ignoring the very real implications of this line of inquiry. A question rendered unanswerable by the insistent a priori assumptions from which it was derived.

If humans are intrinsically beneficent, conscientious, fair, humble, and honest then why is football rife with racism, misogyny, greed, disenfranchisement, lies, cheating, violence, and the hypocrisy to deny the occurrence of any such behaviors except when a rival club is the guilty party? Behaviors which are perpetrated by individual supporters, fanatical groups, club administrations, corporate bodies such as FIFA, political entities, nations, and even the players themselves. No one participating faction is guiltless.

To so blithely sidestep more sufficient explanations for the empirical reality of wickedness in football and in human behavior more generally is not just factually incorrect. It undermines real attempts to understand the root of these behaviors where they might be dealt with more effectively.

To listen to The Moral Maze episode entitled “Football: a moral force for good?” produced by BBC radio and featuring PortuGOAL’s Seleção correspondent Nathan Motz click on this link

Furthermore, in good faith, how successful is a response against heinous evil when those perpetrating it are regarded as behaving in accord with their unique moral ambitions? Humans refuse to accept their indisputably objective moral lacking at a fundamental level. This restricts us from ever doing any more than “stamping out” the alleged impropriety of others – while we ourselves may readily engage in these very same behaviors. Moral relativism cannot account for the moral outrage emanating from all corners of the football world. Is Qatar wrong to perpetrate human rights atrocities or is it allowable because their culture is indifferent? Is hooliganism objectively wrong, or only when someone else’s club is responsible?

The overlooking of our own failings while lashing out at others for theirs is quintessential to the morally fallen nature of humanity itself. Football tells all. There are not good people and bad people. There are only people who both willfully and unintentionally transgress the moral code embedded in each of our hearts. A moral law which can only have ever been given by a moral Lawgiver. Otherwise, it is truly your word against mine as it pertains to the moral validity of all behaviors - even those which may deeply violate your conscience.

Football generates an episodic, semantic, and imagistic description of universal archetypes: good and evil, sacrifice and greed, cruelty and mercy, hero and anti-hero. It speaks to every arc of life’s plotline: tragedy, comedy, victory, defeat, the finite nature of our circumstances. It is one of the preeminent existential dialects alongside religion, visual art, written and spoken language, music, and theatre. Football plucks a primal chord.

As metaphysics have been curtailed to just physics in the modern secular West, football has only elevated our awareness of the often dire moral predicaments of our species. Yet it is clear the moral conversation does not originate on a football pitch. The pitch is but a grand stage upon which moral themes may be observed and critiqued, a mirror held in front of our own soul.

The desire for ultimate purpose, for meaning beyond the material realm, never disappeared in modernity. Arguably, it has only become more desperate as the outcry for ultimate significance comes under more scrutiny and criticism by those who constrain ontological explanations to those accounted for by physics. The severity of the mental health crisis in the West is in no small part explained by the terrifying existential awareness we are cut adrift in an abstract sea of meaningless, hopeless wandering between two oblivions - that before mortal life began and that at mortal life’s end. Let football be one voice which reassures you - there is ultimate meaning and good reasons why football so unsettles our craving for justice.

In a palpable, unavoidable way, football and supporter communities across Europe are encountering moral quandaries. In Portugal, age-old debates between FC Porto and Benfica have routinely been haunted by, in many cases, farcical and cynical hypocrisy. Throughout its history, the FPF has been susceptible to showing partiality to a club, player, or style which ultimately degrades the performance of our beloved Seleção. Deny it all we like, and not one aspect of this reality is changed.

In conclusion, whatever your thoughts or beliefs on the subject of moral significance, its origin, the meaning of life, and how football draws that out, I hope it will be beneficial to examine these matters more deeply. In my zeal, I admit I have not always been prudent to initiate these discussions in a way which respects thoughtful disagreement. It is my hope this effort to bring that discussion to Portuguese football will be well-received and carefully pondered. But I will contentedly listen to those followers who may be genuinely unhappy or unsupportive of this or any other idea about which I write.

The readers of this website and the larger Portuguese football loving world are my people. It is a joy and a responsibility I take seriously whenever I have the opportunity to raise important questions about the Selecao, the game, and our lives in general.

#2385. "We hope he makes the right choice" – Mancini on Tresoldi's decision between Germany and Italy

Eligible to represent both countries, Nicolo Tresoldi remains undecided over whether to play for Germany or Italy at senior international level.

The 22-year-old German U21 international has so far made himself unavailable for selection by either senior national team as he carefully considers which country to represent.

"His father is Italian; he won the Euros with the U21s. I think there's a chance. He knows he can be an important player for the future; we hope he makes the right choice."

Tresoldi is currently with the German U21 national team, preparing for their upcoming games against Belgium and Croatia.

A late equaliser for Fortuna Sittard in De Kuip proved costly for Feyenoord as PSV and Twente racked up huge victories to move within two points of the Eredivisie leaders.

Second-placed Ajax are a point behind after a narrow win at home against AZ ended an 11-match winless streak against the Alkmaar club, as Tolu Arokodare’s early header proved decisive.

Twente recorded the biggest win of the weekend with a 9-0 demolition of Excelsior in Rotterdam, with Wout Weghorst scoring four times and Sondre Ørjasaeter notching up five assists.

Excelsior collapsed following the fifth-minute dismissal of Nesto Groen for a reckless high challenge. The home side were 5-0 down at half-time, with Weghorst claiming a hat-trick in the opening 33 minutes.

PSV bagged seven goals in a one-sided encounter with bottom club Willem II, with all the goals coming from different scorers including Ricardo Pepi, who came on in the second half after a six-week absence through injury.

Telstar opened up a three-point gap at the bottom over ADO Den Haag with a 4-2 win against Utrecht in a painful return to Velsen-Zuid for Telstar’s former coach Anthony Correia.

ADO went down 1-0 in Zwolle, with PEC’s Dylan Mbayo scoring the only goal with a first-time finish on the stroke of half-time.

The match between Cambuur Leeuwarden and Go Ahead Eagles was suspended in the 82nd minute after visiting fans threw fireworks onto the pitch.

The clubs agreed with the KNVB not to play the final minutes and allow Cambuur’s 4-0 advantage to stand as the final result.

Go Ahead’s director Jan-Willem van Dop said the club would take action against those responsible: “I am ashamed on behalf a small group of supporters who ruined things for the club and a large hare of the fans in the away end,” he said.

#2380. Dual passports issue continues to plague Dutch-based footballers

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Dutch-born footballers who were called up to play for former colonies such as Suriname and Indonesia are still in limbo over their national status, six months after the “passportgate” crisis first flared up.

Suriname’s parliament passed a law in September enabling players who are selected for the national team to gain citizenship automatically.

But the Dutch immigration service IND said the law passed in Suriname made no difference to the rules on dual nationality in the Netherlands.

“The fact that Surinamese nationality has been obtained by legal means is not enough on its own to qualify for one of the exceptional categories under our law,” a spokesman for the Dutch justice ministry told broadcaster NOS.

The law was intended to settle the question of dual nationality for footballers, which arose last March when NAC Breda challenged the eligibility of Go Ahead Eagles defender Dean James.

James took Indonesian citizenship last year after accepting an invitation to play for the national team, but under Dutch dual nationality rules he was required to give up his original passport.

The Dutch football association KNVB launched an investigation into the employment status of players who were called up by countries such as Suriname, Cape Verde and Indonesia. In the meantime it arranged for Dutch-based players to be given temporary work permits so they could keep playing for their clubs.

Losing Dutch nationality also stripped them of their EU citizenship, meaning they would need to obtain visas and meet stricter criteria, such as an annual salary threshold of €600,000, to be eligible to play in the Eredivisie.

Several players have passed up invitations to join the Surinamese squad because of the uncertainty, including Danilho Doekhi of Lazio, Milan van Ewijk of Coventry City and Jayden Oosterwolde of Fenerbahçe.

National team manager Henk Fraser said it was “inexplicable” that players’ livelihoods were being damaged by their desire to play international football.

“Both countries have to get together and clarify the situation,” he told NOS. “This is a straightforward case of people following their hearts and wanting to play for Suriname, but not knowing where they stand.”

Some players said they felt misled by the Surinamese football association, which told them they were playing for the national team on “sports passports” that allowed them to remain Dutch citizens.

Former Feyenoord winger Jean-Paul Boëtius said he only discovered after he was first called up for Suriname last year that he had inadvertently renounced his Dutch citizenship as a result.

Shortly afterwards he received a letter from the city council in Rotterdam telling him to hand in his Dutch passport, which he had recently renewed, because it was no longer valid.

“I now have a sticker in my Surinamese passport that says I have a residency permit allowing me to live in the Netherlands,” he told NOS. “But that’s only for a year. It’s a very difficult situation. I want to become Dutch again. I was born and brought up here.”

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Una cifra simile nel calcio europeo sarebbe stata irraggiungibile, un dettaglio non trascurabile che aiuta notevolmente a digerire questa nuova avventura esotica.

"Après trois éliminations successives en Coupe de France aux tirs au but contre La Duchère (1-1, 3-4 t.a.b.), GOAL FC (1-1, 3-4 t.a.b.) et Bourg-en-Bresse (3-3, 3-4 tirs au but),   le GFA Rumilly Vallières a enfin brisé la spirale infernale. Gardien belge de 29 ans arrivé cet été de Colmar (N1), Valentin Baume a stoppé 3 tirs au but de Boron (R1) et envoyé le demi-finaliste de 2021 au 7e tour. "C’est un exercice qu’il maîtrise grâce à son instinct", le félicitait Cédric Rullier. L’histoire le prouve avec un penalty stoppé l’an dernier pour Colmar. Mais surtout ce moment de grâce vécu 6 ans plus tôt en Coupe de Belgique avec Francs Borains où il était entré en plein match suite à l’expulsion du titulaire pour repousser le penalty sans s’échauffer et finir par qualifier les siens."

#2374. Feyenoord facing fireworks fine after 3-0 loss to Internazionale

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Feyenoord are facing a heavy fine from European football authorities after fireworks were let off during their Champions League defeat by Internazionale.

Referee Glenn Nyberg suspended the match for several minutes after flares were fired onto the roof from outside De Kuip stadium, damaging the structure. Police later made two arrests.

“It’s obviously not right and it’ll be another fine,” coach Giovanni van Bronckhorst said wearily after the game, which ended in a comprehensive 3-0 defeat for his team.

One flare landed on the pitch despite the club taking measures such as hanging nets from the gantries to stop objects being thrown from the stands. “They’re letting off fireworks from outside now,” Van Bronckhorst said. “What do you do about that?”

Uefa are likely to hit Feyenoord with a fine and could order the club to close parts of the stadium for their next home matches in the competition, against Porto on November 24 and RB Leipzig on January 27.

The club also have a suspended penalty hanging over them from last October, when they were fined €60,000 after fans let off fireworks during the match against Greek club Panathinaikos.

On the pitch, Feyenoord were undone by a classic display of Italian defending and counter-attacking, as Internazionale sat back after taking an eighth-minute lead through Yann Bisseck.

Francesco Pio Esposito headed in a second early in the second half, just as Feyenoord were pushing for an equaliser, before Piotr Zielinski made sure of the win with a shot from 20 metres.

Captain Luciano Valente said the result was “more than deserved” and the visitors had shown more “quality”.

“This defeat was harder to take than the one against Barcelona,” he told Ziggo Sport. “Because we thought going in that we could get something, but we just weren’t good enough.”

Van Bronckhorst said he was not surprised by the difference in class between the teams. “They scored the first goal out of nothing,” he said. “And Inter know better what the game needs at every stage.

“Their players have a lot of experience, they’ve been in the final twice in the last four years. So we’re at a disadvantage.”

#2371. Cyril Martin-Pichon : "Content d'affronter les Verts !"

Ancien capitaine de la réserve stéphanoise, Cyril Martin-Pichon s'est confié à Poteaux Carrés avant d'affronter son club formateur ce dimanche après-midi à Annecy lors du 7e tour de Coupe de France. Dans cette première partie de l'entretien, le milieu de terrain du GFA Rumilly-Vallières revient sur ses vertes années.

La Piche, comment as-tu vécu ce tirage au sort ? Tu souhaitais tomber sur Sainté ? Tu pressentais que tu allais retrouver les Verts ? Je suis super content de ce tirage. Super content ! Quand j’ai vu les groupes, je me suis dit : « pourquoi pas tomber sur Sainté ? » Pour moi c’était le meilleur tirage ! Peut-être que dans l'absolu ça aurait été mieux de jouer une équipe plus petite pour avoir plus de chances de continuer. Mais non en fait, je suis content. En vrai, tout le monde était content du tirage. Ça va être une belle fête !Tu sens qu’il y a une certaine excitation au sein de ton club et de son environnement à quelques jours de cette affiche ? Oui, c'est sûr. On va jouer contre les leaders de Ligue 2, et au-delà de ça, les Verts c'est un club mythique. Mais on a un match de championnat à jouer ce mercredi contre Saint-Priest avant ce match de Coupe de France. Chaque chose en son temps. L’affiche de dimanche, c’est beaucoup de plaisir, ça a amené du sourire à beaucoup de personnes, surtout des bénévoles qui sont supporters des Verts. Le président aussi, ça fait un moment qu'il nous demande d'essayer de faire un parcours en Coupe pour taper un gros. Tout le monde est content ! On a un ou deux membres du staff qui sont supporters des Verts. Je pense notamment à notre analyste vidéo, Arnaud Morel. Le président du club supporte également les Verts. Son fils aussi. Parmi les bénévoles, je vais citer un gars qui s’appelle Pino, c’est un vrai supporter des Verts. Bruno Piccon, c’est pareil. Il connaît bien la famille Chambost. Et puis il y a moi quelque part ! Bon, on ne va pas se mentir, je ne suis pas un fervent supporter mais évidemment les Verts ne me laissent pas insensible. L’ASSE, c’est mon club formateur, j’y ai passé plusieurs années. Je suis forcément les résultats et je regarde les résumés des matches. Et puis mon père est supporter des Verts. A l’époque où Léo et Dylan étaient encore au club, je regardais un peu plus les matches, j’ai trouvé ça top qu’ils contribuent tous les deux à faire monter les Verts en L1. A un niveau plus modeste, on avait connu ensemble les joies d’une montée avec la réserve 6 ans auparavant. Tu as défendu nos couleurs 4 ans et demi, de juillet 2015 à janvier 2020. Que gardes-tu de cette expérience ? Franchement, que du positif ! Que ce soit la formation, le passage dans le monde adulte ou même avec les pros. J’ai fait de belles rencontres. Je ne suis pas du tout revanchard comme certains le sont.Tu as joué sous les ordres de Lionel Vaillant et Gilles Rodriguez en U17, sous la houlette de Jean-Philippe Primard puis Julien Sablé en U19, sous la direction de Laurent Batlles puis Razik Nedder en équipe réserve. Lequel de ces entraîneurs t’aura le plus marqué ?Sans hésiter, Laurent Batlles. De loin. Je pense que c'est l’entraîneur qui m'a fait le plus progresser. C’est avec lui que j’ai entretenu les meilleures relations, que le courant passait le mieux. C'est avec lui aussi qu'on a eu les meilleurs résultats, j'ai envie de dire. Même si je n’oublie pas qu’en U19 avec Jean-Philippe Primard, on a fait une très belle saison [en 2015-2016, ndp2]. On avait fini en tête de notre groupe après avoir battu l’OM 4-0 à Marseille. Mais en demi-finale, on s’était incliné sur le même score à la maison contre une grosse équipe du PSG. Je me souviens qu’en face il y avait Ikoné, Nkunku, Jean-Kévin Augustin, Odsonne Edouard. Paris avait une sacrée attaque !Mais j’ai passé deux années vraiment top quand Laurent Batlles entraînait la réserve. La première année, on est monté en N2 en finissant premiers du groupe. Et la seconde on s’est maintenu sans trembler. C’est la période où j’ai pris le plus de plaisir dans le jeu. J’ai beaucoup appris sous les ordres du coach Batlles. Il m’a notamment fait progresser dans mon agressivité, que ce soit avec le ballon ou sans ballon. J’ai passé un cap avec lui. J’ai pris du plaisir dans son équipe. J'avais peut-être un peu plus de maturité dans mon jeu, vu que c'est la première fois que je découvrais le monde adulte.Tu auras beaucoup moins joué avec Razik Nedder. Après quelques mois avec lui tu as quitté le club à mi-saison pour retourner à Bourgoin, ta ville natale. Tu sentais que ton avenir était bouché à Sainté ? J’ai signé pro quand on est monté de N3 à N2. J’avais repris avec les pros, fait la prépa avec eux. J’avais joué les matches amicaux de l’équipe première, je me souviens qu’on avait battu Lens, Marseille, Nice… Mais ensuite j’ai joué la saison avec la réserve. Ensuite, Laurent Batlles est parti à Troyes et c’est Razik qui a récupéré la réserve tandis que Ghislain Printant a succédé à Jean-Louis Gasset à la tête de l’équipe première. Je me suis entraîné un peu avec les pros et j’ai joué 4 mois avec la réserve, j’ai même porté le brassard de capitaine quelques matches. Mais la volonté du club était de faire monter pas mal de jeunes qui avaient gagné la Gambardella quelques mois plus tôt. Razik et le staff de Puel m’ont fait comprendre qu’il valait mieux que je trouve un autre projet… J’aurais pu rester car j’avais encore 18 mois de contrat. Mais moi j’avais besoin de jouer et de retrouver du plaisir. Qu'est-ce qui t'a manqué à ton avis pour t’imposer chez les pros ? Il y avait des joueurs devant moi qui étaient plus que confirmés. Difficile de rivaliser avec un Yann M’Vila par exemple. Mahdi Camara montait en puissance. Ce n’était pas évident de se mettre à leur niveau. Après, j'aurais pu, je pense, mentalement, un peu plus m'accrocher. Je pense que mentalement, je n'étais pas encore prêt. Si j’avais eu un peu plus la dalle, j’aurais peut-être pu gratter des choses.Quel est le meilleur joueur que tu aies côtoyé à Sainté ?Au centre de formation, j’ai côtoyé beaucoup de bons joueurs mais il y en a un que j'aime particulièrement parce qu’on parle le même football, c'est Dylan Chambost. J'ai toujours aimé ce profil de joueur. Il a un pied gauche exceptionnel. Et puis, il est beau à voir jouer. Il a la classe, je trouve. Dans un registre différent, j’aime beaucoup Mahdi Camara. Je l'ai vu évoluer. D'une saison à une autre, il a pris en maturité, en confiance en lui. C’est un joueur qui a toujours eu la dalle. A un moment donné il a passé un cap techniquement et dans la maturité aussi. Et vraiment, à ce moment-là, il m'a impressionné. Après, je citerais Allan Saint-Maximin. Lui, c'était un phénomène. Et sur la fin, j’ai comme tout le monde été impressionné par William Saliba. Il était surclassé avec nous. Chez les pros, le joueur qui m’aura le plus marqué est Yann M’Vila. Même en dehors du terrain, c’était la classe. Il était hyper gentil, de bon conseil et bienveillant avec les jeunes. Sur le rectangle vert, il m’a impressionné par sa qualité de passe, sa lecture du jeu, son placement. Il était toujours bien placé, ne perdait pas un ballon, en récupérait beaucoup. Je le trouvais très fort.Quels étaient tes meilleurs potes au centre de formation, ceux dont tu étais le plus proche et que tu côtoyais le plus en dehors des terrains ? Les deux années où j’ai joué avec eux en réserve dans l’équipe entraînée par Laurent Batlles, j’étais proche de Léo Pétrot, Dylan Chambost et Mickaël Panos. J’étais beaucoup avec Adrien Fleury aussi. Après je m'entendais super bien avec Mickaël Nadé, Ronaël Pierre-Gabriel. Je continue de suivre le beau parcours de tout ce beau monde mais de loin, forcément ! On s’envoie à l’occasion des petits messages. A l’époque de Laurent Batlles, on avait vraiment une belle équipe réserve. Je me réjouis de la réussite de mes anciens coéquipiers. Quand tu vois que Mahdi a brillé en Ligue des Champions avec Brest, que Léo performe cette saison en Europa League avec Anderlecht, que Dylan s’éclate en MLS... Je suis content pour eux. Quel est ton meilleur souvenir à Sainté ? Sur le plan collectif, les années Batlles, incontestablement ! On va dire la montée en N2. Une montée, c'est toujours agréable. Sur le plan personnel, c'est ma signature professionnelle, et puis l'été 2018 quand j’ai joué avec les pros les matches de préparation.Quel est le plus mauvais souvenir de tes vertes années ? C'est quand je suis parti, je pense ! Mais c’est surtout la tragique disparition de mon coéquipier William Gomis [victime d'une fusillade à La Seyne-sur-Mer il y a 8 ans, ndp2]. Il n’avait même pas 20 ans. Perdre la vie aussi jeune, dans de telles circonstances… Je n’ai pas les mots. On a tous été choqués, bouleversés. Peu de temps avant de décéder, il avait vécu un petit moment chez moi. Je l’avais hébergé, le temps qu’il trouve un appart. William, on ne t’oublie pas !

Réagir sur le forumLe débat continue entre potonautes.

Le retour du Derby à GG après 3 ans d'absence. Encore un match d'anthologie !

#2369. PSV edged out by sharper Club Brugge in Champions League defeat

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

PSV Eindhoven endured a frustrating night in the Champions League as their defensive frailties were exposed in a 2-1 defeat by Club Brugge.

PSV started full of attacking intent and should have taken an early lead when Ivan Perisic put Sergiño Dest clean through on goal, but the defender’s shot was too close to goalkeeper Yann Sommer.

Instead it was the visitors who took the lead with their first real attack when Nicolo Tresoldi headed in a teasing cross from Joaquin Seys.

PSV equalised early in the second half as an outstanding pass by Sven Mijnans into the path Lutsharel Geertruida allowed the defender to charge to the byline. His pullback found its way to Kodai Sano, whose shot took a deflection off Seys into the net.

But five minutes later PSV were caught out as another razor-sharp pass by Seys released Carlos Forbs in space to net the winner.

The home side refused to give up, but were unable to find a way past Sommer in the closing stages as the Belgian side closed out the win.

Team captain Mauro Junior admitted that PSV had been undone by shortcomings at the back. “We had a lot of opportunities in the first half but we didn’t score,” he told Ziggo Sport.

“We want to attack and score goals, but we weren’ well organised today in defence. We need to keep our heads up and look forward to the next game.”

The result leaves PSV in 24th place, just above the elimination line, ahead of their next match away to Real Madrid on November 24, with four points from their opening four matches.

Is AZ Alkmaar's academy now the best in the Netherlands? ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 4 of the Eredivisie ...

It's time for our BIG Dutch Eredivisie season preview! Football-Oranje's ...

A new season of Dutch football is here! Football-Oranje's Michael ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul preview the Netherlands' Nations League game ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

An injury-hit Netherlands side battled back to draw 2-2 against Greece in the Nations League.

Xavi was forced into changes with Cody Gakpo and Jan Paul van Hecke dropping out of the squad. Tijjani Reijnders and Ryan Gravenberch also dropped to the bench with Quinten Timber, Guus Til, Jurrien Timber and Ruben van Bommel starting.

Greece have been in good form recently and their confidence was clear from the start. They had a goal disallowed for offside before Fotis Ioannidis made it 1-0 after 23 minutes.

The Netherlands offered little going forward and in stoppage time, a bad back pass from Van Bommel led to Giorgos Masouras sliding in a second.

Xavi made a triple change as Gravenberch, Noa Lang and Reijnders came on and they all made a big difference as the Netherlands dominated the second half.

Virgil van Dijk headed one back before Denzel Dumfries had two goals disallowed for offside. Xavi turned to Joshua Zirkzee and debutante Gjivai Zechiel in hope of an equaliser.

The equaliser came in the last minute as Zechiel’s overhead kick dropped into the path of Reijnders who blasted the ball in. Zechiel then came close to a winner but his header was straight at the goalkeeper.

A battling point for the Netherlands, who stay unbeaten under Xavi with a game at home to Serbia to come on Sunday.

Entraîneur-adjoint du GFA Rumilly-Vallières, qui joue demain en N1 contre Saint-Priest dans la banlieue de la banlieue avant de recevoir les Verts dimanche à Annecy en Coupe de France, Damien Guerrier s'est confié au Dauphiné Libéré. Extraits.

"À la suite de notre succès obtenu contre Istres (5-0) le 31 octobre, on avait décidé de laisser 4 jours de repos complet aux joueurs, étant donné que nous étions exempts en championnat ce week-end. C’était le moment idéal pour couper un peu, se régénérer, après 18 semaines consécutives de travail. Certains en ont profité pour partir quelques jours, d’autres sont restés dans le coin. Les séances ont été très bonnes, les gars avaient du jus. La particularité de cette semaine, c’est qu’il n’y a pas un mais deux matches à préparer. La charge de travail est donc plus conséquente."

#2366. Lazio-Napoli, Fusco su Pedraza: «poteva anche essere rivisto»

Pur esprimendo dubbi sull’azione del gol azzurro, Fusco considera giusto il pareggio e valuta positivamente il punto.

Alessandro Fusco considera giusto il pareggio della Lazio contro il Napoli, ma ha un dubbio sull’azione del gol azzurro: a suo giudizio, Pedraza sarebbe stato spinto e l’episodio avrebbe potuto essere rivisto dall’arbitro. Intervenuto a Radio Radio, Fusco ha analizzato la gara del Maradona e la prestazione della squadra di Gattuso.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

«Pareggio giusto, la Lazio ha dominato il primo tempo», ha detto Fusco. Secondo la sua analisi, i biancocelesti hanno commesso errori sotto misura: Isaksen e Taylor potevano e dovevano cercare più spesso il tiro.

Nei primi 20 minuti della ripresa, invece, il Napoli si è guadagnato il pareggio. Fusco ritiene che il risultato dia morale agli azzurri, pur senza consentire loro di recuperare punti in classifica.

Il dubbio di Fusco riguarda l’autogol di Pedraza. Nell’azione, a suo avviso, c’è stato un contatto ai danni del giocatore della Lazio: «Per me sul gol del Napoli c’è un contatto su Pedraza, poteva anche essere rivisto, perché viene spinto vistosamente».

La sua è una valutazione sull’episodio: Fusco sostiene che l’arbitro avrebbe potuto esaminarlo di nuovo.

#2365. Bayern remain in talks to sign Liverpool's Rio Ngumoha

Liverpool youngster Rio Ngumoha was one of the options Bayern Munich explored last summer as the German champions searched for a new winger.

According to multiple reports, Bayern were ready to splurge around €70 million on the 18-year-old, but the move ultimately didn't materialise due to Liverpool's reluctance to sell, with the German champions instead going on to sign Ismael Saibari from PSV.

In quotes published on his CfBayern Insider website, Bild's head of football Christian Falk has now shed further light on Bayern's interest in Ngumoha, claiming that the Bavarians remain in contact with the youngster's camp.

“There were talks, his brother talked with FC Bayern, but it was a difficult situation for Bayern Munich. It was not clear if Liverpool would sell him or not. The price wouldn’t have been extremely high. So, you see, the club’s decision-makers feel he’d be a great option for Bayern Munich, which is why Ngumoha is still on Bayern’s shortlist," Falk said.

“In the end, they had to decide for Ismael Saibari. Rio would have been complicated, so they just looked to seal the comparatively easier transfer of the two. Bayern are still in talks with Ngumoha’s camp, however, and he could be an option for the summer.”

The young winger has made 14 appearances for Liverpool in all competitions this season, providing two assists.

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

It's time for our BIG Dutch Eredivisie season preview! Football-Oranje's ...

A new season of Dutch football is here! Football-Oranje's Michael ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul preview the Netherlands' Nations League game ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

The Netherlands came back from 2-0 down to earn a 2-2 draw against Greece in the Nations League. Here is our player ratings for the Netherlands players.

Bart Verbruggen 5.5/10: Wasn’t happy with his defence in the first half. Could have done more for the first goal but did make a good save. Had nothing to do after the break.

Denzel Dumfries 5/10: Struggled in the first half before improving in the second. Had two goals disallowed for offside.

Micky van de Ven 5/10: His pace makes up for the gaps he leaves at times and in the firs half he struggled. Like others he got better in the second but had less to do.

Jurrien Timber 4/10: Looked very much like a player who is not fully fit. Second to everything and gave away fouls in the first half. Was too slow too react for the second goal.

Virgil van Dijk 6/10: Defensively he struggled in the first but his goal got his side back into the game after the break.

Joey Veerman 6.5/10: Always looking to make something happen going forward. Not everything worked but was always available. His corner led to a goal an he almost scored a free kick.

Quinten Timber 4/10: Disastrous display as he gave away foul after foul in the first half. Was rightly hooked at half time.

Guus Til 4/10: Like Timber, he offered nothing in the first half and was rightly taken off. Xavi wanted to give him a chance but he didn’t take it.

Crysencio Summerville 6/10: Was Netherlands only outlet going forward in the first half and is a constant threat on the right hand side.

Ruben van Bommel 4.5/10: Was too static at times and his error led to the second goal. Taken off at the break.

Mexx Meerdink 5/10: Tried but was isolated at times. Had no service in the first half and barely touched the ball in the second.

Noa Lang 7/10: Offered more on the left than Van Bommel as he took the game to the Greek defence. His cross led to the equaliser. Likely to start against Serbia after this display.

Tijjani Reijnders 6.5/10: Came on and scored the equaliser with a good finish. Did a lot more running than Til did and it was much needed energy.

Ryan Gravenberch 6.5/10: Immediately helped the Netherlands midfield gain control back in the second half.

Joshua Zirkzee N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

Gjivai Zechiel N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

FT Greece 2-2 NetherlandsA battling draw after being 2-0 down. Had 2 goals disallowed too! Much better second half but that first half was a disaster

#2363. Baysse en mission chez les Girondins

L'ancien défenseur central stéphanois Paul Baysse (38 ans) est actuellement en mission chez son club formateur, comme il l'explique dans un entretien paru avant-hier dans Le Télégramme. Extraits.

"Depuis juillet, je suis en charge des U17 nationaux des Girondins de Bordeaux. Et en octobre, après le changement de propriétaire, on m’a proposé d’épauler le coach principal, Arnaud Cormier. J’ai mon BEF, donc je suis qualifié pour cette double mission, que j’effectue à titre bénévole, pour donner un coup de main, explique-t-il. L’objectif, c’est à la fois de maintenir nos jeunes au niveau national, en U17 puis en U19 d’ici deux ans, et à côté de remonter les échelons avec les seniors [rélégués administrativement en Régional 1, ndp2].

Notre équipe fanion a bien débuté son championnat : quatre victoires en autant de matchs. Face au Stade Montois, le 25 octobre, on a battu le record d’affluence de la division avec plus de 11 000 spectateurs réuni, preuve que l’engouement est toujours là. Cela fait 4 ans que j’ai arrêté de jouer, mon projet c’est de devenir dirigeant. Je suis la formation en management du sport au CDES de Limoges et si pour l’instant j’ai un rôle de terrain, j’aimerais évoluer vers des postes à responsabilités. On verra si c’est possible. À Bordeaux, ou ailleurs."
